==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/Admin ====


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/Admin/DepartmentRequestsAdminController.cs ====
This C# file defines the `DepartmentRequestsAdminController`, which provides administrative endpoints for managing department creation requests within the E-Learning system.

### **Key Classes**
*   **`DepartmentRequestsAdminController`**: An API controller restricted to users with the **"Admin"** role. It handles the lifecycle of department requests.
*   **`DepartmentRequestRejectDTO`**: A simple data transfer object used to receive an optional rejection note from the client.

### **Key Functions & Logic**
*   **`List` (GET `api/admin/department-requests`)**: 
    *   Retrieves a paginated list of department requests.
    *   Supports optional filtering by `universityId` and `status`.
*   **`Approve` (POST `{id}/approve`)**: 
    *   Approves a specific request identified by `id`.
    *   Extracts the administrator's ID from the current user's claims to track who performed the action.
*   **`Reject` (POST `{id}/reject`)**: 
    *   Rejects a specific request.
    *   Accepts a `Note` from the request body explaining the reason for rejection.
    *   Also tracks the administrator's ID.

### **Dependencies & Implementation**
*   **Service Layer**: It relies on `IDepartmentRequestService` to execute the underlying business logic and database operations.
*   **Security**: Enforces strict authorization via the `[Authorize(Roles = "Admin")]` attribute and uses `JwtMiddleware` (implied by claim extraction) for identity management.
This code defines the **`DepartmentRequestsAdminController`**, which provides administrative endpoints for managing department creation or modification requests within the system.

### **Key Classes**
*   **`DepartmentRequestsAdminController`**: An API controller restricted to users with the **"Admin"** role. It handles incoming HTTP requests related to department request management.
*   **`DepartmentRequestRejectDTO`**: A simple data transfer object used to receive an optional rejection note from the request body.

### **Key Functions & Logic**
1.  **`List` (GET `api/admin/department-requests`)**:
    *   Retrieves a paginated list of department requests.
    *   Supports optional filtering by `universityId` and `status`.
    *   Returns the data provided by the `IDepartmentRequestService`.

2.  **`Approve` (POST `{id}/approve`)**:
    *   Approves a specific department request.
    *   Extracts the `userId` from the current authenticated Admin's claims to track who performed the action.
    *   Delegates the logic to the service layer's `ApproveRequest` method.

3.  **`Reject` (POST `{id}/reject`)**:
    *   Rejects a specific department request.
    *   Requires a `DepartmentRequestRejectDTO` containing an optional note explaining the rejection.
    *   Extracts the `adminId` from the user claims and passes it along with the rejection note to the service layer.

### **Core Logic Patterns**
*   **Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)**: Enforced via the `[Authorize(Roles = "Admin")]` attribute at the class level.
*   **Service Layer Delegation**: The controller does not contain business logic; it acts as a thin wrapper around `IDepartmentRequestService`.
*   **Standardized Responses**: Returns consistent JSON objects indicating success or failure (`success: true/false`) along with relevant data or error messages.
*   **Claim Extraction**: Uses `User.FindFirst("userId")` to identify the administrative actor for auditing or processing purposes.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/Admin/UniversityRequestsAdminController.cs ====
The `UniversityRequestsAdminController.cs` file defines an administrative API controller for managing university registration or update requests. It is restricted to users with the **"Admin"** role.

### **Key Class**
*   **`UniversityRequestsAdminController`**: A RESTful controller inheriting from `ControllerBase` that handles administrative actions related to university requests.

### **Dependencies**
*   **`IUniversityRequestService`**: The primary service used to perform business logic such as fetching, approving, or rejecting requests.

### **Primary Endpoints & Logic**

1.  **`List` (GET `api/admin/university-requests`)**
    *   **Purpose**: Retrieves a paginated list of university requests.
    *   **Parameters**: Supports optional filtering by `status` and pagination via `page` and `pageSize`.
    *   **Logic**: Calls the service's `GetRequests` method and returns the data or a `BadRequest` if the service fails.

2.  **`Approve` (POST `api/admin/university-requests/{id}/approve`)**
    *   **Purpose**: Approves a pending university request by its ID.
    *   **Logic**: 
        *   Extracts the current Admin's `userId` from the JWT claims.
        *   Calls `_service.ApproveRequest` to finalize the approval and create/update the university record.
        *   Returns the resulting university data on success.

3.  **`Reject` (POST `api/admin/university-requests/{id}/reject`)**
    *   **Purpose**: Rejects a university request with a reason/note.
    *   **Logic**: 
        *   Extracts the rejection `note` from the request body.
        *   Extracts the current Admin's `userId` from the JWT claims.
        *   Calls `_service.RejectRequest` to update the request status to "Rejected."

### **Common Logic Patterns**
*   **Authorization**: Uses the `[Authorize(Roles = "Admin")]` attribute to ensure only administrators can access these endpoints.
*   **Response Handling**: Uses a standard result pattern (`res.Success`) to determine whether to return an `Ok` response with data or a `BadRequest` with an error message.
*   **User Identification**: Manually parses the `id` claim from the `User` context to track which administrator performed the action.
This `UniversityRequestsAdminController` provides an administrative API for managing university affiliation requests. It is restricted to users with the **"Admin"** role and routes through `api/admin/university-requests`.

### **Key Classes & Logic**
*   **`UniversityRequestsAdminController`**: A controller that handles HTTP requests for listing, approving, and rejecting university registration/affiliation requests.
*   **Service Dependency**: It delegates all business logic to `IUniversityRequestService`.
*   **Authorization**: Uses the `[Authorize(Roles = "Admin")]` attribute to ensure only administrators can access these endpoints.

### **Key Functions**
*   **`List` (GET)**: Retrieves a paginated list of university requests. It allows optional filtering by `status` (e.g., "Pending", "Approved", "Rejected") and supports pagination via `page` and `pageSize`.
*   **`Approve` (POST `{id}/approve`)**: Marks a specific request as approved. It extracts the administrator's user ID from the JWT claims to track who performed the action. On success, it returns the newly created or updated university data.
*   **`Reject` (POST `{id}/reject`)**: Rejects a request. It accepts an optional `note` from the request body to explain the reason for rejection and, like the approve method, tracks the administrator's ID.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/AdminController.cs ====
The `AdminController.cs` file defines the `AdminController` class, which handles administrative operations for the E-Learning platform. It is a protected API controller requiring the **Admin** role for most operations.

### Key Class & Dependencies
- **`AdminController`**: Inherits from `ControllerBase`, providing RESTful API endpoints for system administration.
- **`IAdminService`**: Injected via constructor, delegating the core business logic to this service.

### Core Logic & Functions

#### 1. Teacher Approval Management
Handles the lifecycle of teacher applications:
- **`GetPendingTeacherApprovals`**: Retrieves a paginated list of users waiting for teacher status approval.
- **`GetPendingTeacherApprovalsCount`**: Returns the total number of pending teacher requests.
- **`ApproveTeacherRequest`**: Promotes a user to a teacher role.
- **`RejectTeacherRequest`**: Denies a teacher application with a specified reason.

#### 2. Dashboard & Statistics
Provides data for the administrative dashboard:
- **`GetPlatformStats`**: Fetches high-level platform metrics (e.g., total users, courses).
- **`GetRecentActivities`**: Retrieves a paginated log of recent system events.
- **`GetTopCourses`**: Lists the most popular or highest-performing courses.

#### 3. User Management
Allows administrators to manage platform users:
- **`GetAllUsers`**: Lists all users with optional filtering by role or status.
- **`UpdateUserRole`**: Updates the permission level/role of a specific user.

#### 4. Course Approval Workflow
Manages the quality control of new content:
- **`GetPendingCourses`**: Retrieves courses submitted by teachers that are awaiting review.
- **`ApproveCourse`**: Makes a pending course live on the platform.
- **`RejectCourse`**: Sends a course back to the teacher with feedback for improvement.

### Security & Validation
- **Role-Based Access Control**: Enforced via `[Authorize(Roles = "Admin")]`.
- **Identity Verification**: Extracts `adminId` from the JWT token to verify the requester's identity.
- **Error Handling**: Uses standardized `ActionResult` responses (`Ok`, `BadRequest`, `Unauthorized`) based on the success of the service calls.
The `AdminController.cs` file is a Web API controller that provides administrative management features for the E-Learning platform. It is restricted to users with the **"Admin"** role and relies on the `IAdminService` for business logic.

### **Core Components**

*   **Class:** `AdminController`
*   **Access Control:** `[Authorize(Roles = "Admin")]` — Ensures only administrators can access these endpoints.
*   **Dependency:** `IAdminService` — Handles the underlying data operations and business rules.

### **Key Functional Areas**

#### **1. Teacher Approval Management**
*   **`GetPendingTeacherApprovals` / `Count`**: Retrieves a paginated list and the total count of users who have applied for teacher status.
*   **`ApproveTeacherRequest`**: Promotes a user to the Teacher role.
*   **`RejectTeacherRequest`**: Denies a teacher application, requiring a reason provided via `RejectTeacherRequestDTO`.

#### **2. Platform Dashboard & Analytics**
*   **`GetPlatformStats`**: Provides high-level metrics about the platform (users, courses, etc.).
*   **`GetRecentActivities`**: Fetches a paginated log of recent system events for the admin dashboard.
*   **`GetTopCourses`**: Retrieves a list of the most successful or highly-rated courses.

#### **3. User Management**
*   **`GetAllUsers`**: Lists all registered users with optional filtering by role and status.
*   **`UpdateUserRole`**: Allows administrators to manually change a user's role (e.g., promoting a Student to an Admin).

#### **4. Course Moderation**
*   **`GetPendingCourses`**: Displays courses submitted by teachers that are awaiting review.
*   **`ApproveCourse` / `RejectCourse`**: Allows admins to either publish a course to the platform or reject it with feedback.

### **Logic Patterns**
*   **Identity Extraction**: Most methods extract the `adminId` from the JWT token claims to verify the requester's identity.
*   **Standardized Responses**: The controller consistently returns JSON objects containing a `success` flag, a `message`, and the relevant `data` or `errors`.
*   **DTO Usage**: Uses specialized Data Transfer Objects (like `UserFilterDTO`, `ApproveCourseDTO`) to handle complex input validation and filtering.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/AssignmentsController.cs ====
This code defines the `AssignmentsController`, an API controller within the `backend.Controllers` namespace that manages assignment-related data for authenticated users.

### **Key Components**

#### **Class: `AssignmentsController`**
*   **Attributes**: Marked with `[ApiController]` and `[Route("api/[controller]")]`.
*   **Security**: Uses the `[Authorize]` attribute, requiring a valid JWT token for all endpoints.
*   **Dependency Injection**: Injects `ApplicationDbContext` to interact with the database.

#### **Function: `GetUserAssignments()`**
*   **Endpoint**: `GET api/assignments/user`
*   **Purpose**: Retrieves a list of all published assignments and attaches the current user's submission details (if any).
*   **Logic**:
    1.  **User Identification**: Extracts the `userId` from the JWT "userId" claim. Returns `Unauthorized` if the ID is missing or invalid.
    2.  **Assignment Retrieval**: Queries the database for assignments where `IsPublished` is true, including related `Course` titles.
    3.  **Submission Retrieval**: Fetches all `AssignmentSubmissions` specifically belonging to the current user.
    4.  **Data Mapping**: Merges the assignments and submissions in memory. For each assignment, it checks if the user has a corresponding submission and returns an object containing:
        *   Assignment metadata (ID, Title, Due Date, Max Score, Mandatory status).
        *   Course info (ID, Title).
        *   Nested submission info (ID, Score, Status, Submission Date) or `null` if no submission exists.
*   **Response**: Returns a JSON object with a success flag and the formatted list of assignments.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/AuthController.cs ====
This `AuthController` manages user authentication, profile management, and role transitions within the E-Learning platform.

### **Key Class & Dependency**
*   **`AuthController`**: An API controller inheriting from `ControllerBase`, using `IAuthService` to handle core business logic.

### **Core Functionality & Logic**

#### **1. Authentication & Registration**
*   **`Register`**: Validates registration data, converts base64 profile/cover images to server-hosted URLs, and creates a new user.
*   **`Login`**: Authenticates credentials and returns a JWT token along with user details (roles, rank, etc.).

#### **2. Profile Management**
*   **`GetProfile` / `GetPublicUserProfile`**: Retrieves profile details for the authenticated user or a specific public user ID.
*   **`UpdateProfile`**: Allows authenticated users to update personal details and images (handling base64 data URL conversions).
*   **`UploadImage`**: A utility endpoint for uploading raw image files to the server's `wwwroot/Uploads` directory.

#### **3. Account Security**
*   **`ChangePassword`**: Updates the password for the currently logged-in user.
*   **`ForgotPassword`**: Triggers a password reset email/link.
*   **`ResetPassword`**: Validates a reset token and sets a new password.

#### **4. Role & Feature Access**
*   **`BecomeTeacher`**: Elevates the user's status to allow course creation.
*   **`JoinCompetitionMode`**: Enables competitive features for the user account.
*   **`GetDashboard`**: Fetches personalized dashboard data for the authenticated user.

### **Internal Logic (Private Helper)**
*   **`SaveDataUrlToUploads`**: A private method that parses base64 "data:" URLs, saves the binary data as files (PNG, JPG, etc.) in `wwwroot/Uploads`, and returns the accessible public URL.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/ClansController.cs ====
This file defines the **`ClansController`**, an ASP.NET Core API controller responsible for managing clan-related operations, including membership, social features, and administrative tasks.

### **Core Components**
*   **Namespace:** `backend.Controllers`
*   **Dependency Injection:** Uses `IClanService` to handle business logic.
*   **Security:** Implements `[Authorize]` for sensitive operations and extracts the `userId` from JWT claims.

### **Key Logic & Helper Methods**
*   **`CheckClanLeadershipPermissionAsync`:** A private helper that verifies if a user has specific permissions (Leader, CoLeader, or Elder) within a clan before allowing administrative actions.
*   **Leadership Exclusivity:** During clan creation, it enforces a rule that a user cannot create a new clan if they already hold a leadership role in another.

### **Key Functions & Endpoints**

#### **1. Clan Management**
*   **`GetAllClans` / `GetClan`:** Public endpoints to list all clans (paginated) or retrieve specific details by ID.
*   **`CreateClan`:** Allows authenticated users to create a clan, provided they aren't already leaders elsewhere.
*   **`UpdateClan` / `DeleteClan`:** Restricted to leadership (Update) or specifically the Leader (Delete).

#### **2. Membership & Join Requests**
*   **`JoinClan` / `LeaveClan`:** Handles entry (either instant or via a pending request) and exit from a clan.
*   **`GetPendingJoinRequests` / `DecideJoinRequest`:** Allows Leaders and Co-Leaders to view and Approve/Reject pending membership requests.
*   **`UpdateMemberRole` / `RemoveMember`:** Administrative tools for clan leadership to manage their roster.
*   **`GetMyClans`:** Retrieves a list of clans the current user belongs to.

#### **3. Social & Content**
*   **`GetClanPosts` / `CreateClanPost`:** Manages the clan's internal feed.
*   **`ReactToPost` / `VoteOnPost`:** Enables member engagement through emojis and upvotes/downvotes.
*   **`GetClanAnnouncements` / `CreateAnnouncement`:** Provides a broadcast system for Leaders and Co-Leaders to reach all members.

#### **4. Activities & Stats**
*   **`GetClanActivities`:** Retrieves a history of events/actions within the clan.
*   **`GetClanCompetitions`:** Lists competitions associated with the clan, filterable by season.
*   **`GetClanStats`:** (Partial in snippet) Provides performance and data metrics for the clan.
This code snippet belongs to the `ClansController` within an ASP.NET Core Web API, responsible for managing clan-related activities, leaderboards, invitations, and search functionality.

### **Key Class**
*   **`ClansController`**: An API controller that utilizes a `_clanService` to handle business logic and `CheckClanLeadershipPermissionAsync` for authorization checks.

### **Key Functions & Endpoints**

#### **1. Leaderboard & Rankings**
*   **`GetClanLeaderboard` (`GET /leaderboard`)**: Retrieves a paginated list of clans ranked by performance. It supports filtering by timeframe (weekly, monthly, all-time), university, and department.
*   **`GetTopClans` (`GET /top-clans`)**: A simplified endpoint that returns the top 10 clans globally.

#### **2. Clan Invitations (Authenticated)**
*   **`InviteToClan` (`POST /{clanId}/invite/{userId}`)**: Allows clan leaders to invite a specific user. It validates the inviter's leadership permissions before calling the service.
*   **`GetMyInvitations` (`GET /invitations`)**: Fetches all pending clan invitations for the currently logged-in user.
*   **`AcceptInvitation` (`POST /invitations/{invitationId}/accept`)**: Processes a user's acceptance of an invitation, effectively adding them to the clan.
*   **`RejectInvitation` (`POST /invitations/{invitationId}/reject`)**: Allows a user to decline a clan invitation.

#### **3. Clan Interaction**
*   **`ReactToAnnouncement` (`POST /{clanId}/announcements/{announcementId}/react`)**: Enables clan members to add emoji reactions to clan announcements. It verifies that the user is a member of the specific clan before allowing the reaction.

#### **4. Discovery**
*   **`SearchClans` (`GET /search`)**: a robust search endpoint using `ClanSearchFilterDTO`. It allows filtering by name/query, clan type, university, department, ranking range, member count, and public/private status, with support for custom sorting and pagination.

### **Core Logic & Implementation Details**
*   **Authorization**: Most state-changing operations (invites, reactions, accepting/rejecting) are protected by the `[Authorize]` attribute and require a valid JWT token.
*   **Identity Management**: The controller extracts the `userId` from the authenticated user's claims (`User.FindFirst("userId")`).
*   **Service Pattern**: The controller acts as a thin layer, delegating all database operations and complex business rules to `_clanService`.
*   **Error Handling**: Returns standard HTTP status codes:
    *   `200 OK` for successful data retrieval or operations.
    *   `400 BadRequest` for logic failures or invalid model states.
    *   `401 Unauthorized` / `403 Forbid` for missing tokens or insufficient permissions.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/ClanVsClansCompetitionsController.cs ====
This code defines the **`ClanVsClansCompetitionsController`**, an ASP.NET Core API controller responsible for managing the lifecycle of competitive matches between different "clans." It acts as a wrapper around the `IClanVsClansCompetitionService`.

### **Key Class**
*   **`ClanVsClansCompetitionsController`**: Manages HTTP requests for clan-based competitions, handling authentication (via JWT), input validation, and result reporting.

### **Key Functions & Endpoints**

#### **1. Challenge Management**
*   **`CreateCompetition` (POST)**: Initiates a challenge from the current user's clan to an opponent clan.
*   **`AcceptChallenge` / `RejectChallenge` (POST)**: Allows the receiving clan to respond to a pending challenge. Rejections can include a specific reason.
*   **`GetPendingChallenges` (GET)**: Lists all challenges awaiting a response for a specific clan.

#### **2. Participant & Status Setup**
*   **`SelectParticipants` (POST)**: Allows clan leaders to choose which members will participate in the competition.
*   **`ConfirmParticipants` (POST)**: Locks the participant list, signaling readiness.
*   **`StartCompetition` (POST)**: Transitions the competition state from "Scheduled" to "Ongoing."

#### **3. Competition Execution**
*   **`GetCompetitionQuestions` (GET)**: Retrieves the questions assigned to the competition (typically accessible only when the event is active).
*   **`SubmitAnswer` (POST)**: Handles individual participant answer submissions during the event.
*   **`CompleteCompetition` (POST)**: Finalizes the event and triggers result calculations.

#### **4. Data Retrieval & Results**
*   **`GetCompetition` (GET)**: Fetches detailed metadata about a specific competition.
*   **`GetClanCompetitions` (GET)**: Returns a paginated history of competitions (both as challenger and opponent) for a specific clan.
*   **`GetResults` / `GetClanResults` (GET)**: Provides final scores and performance breakdowns for the competition or specific clans.

### **Core Logic & Patterns**
*   **Identity Extraction**: Uses a private helper `GetCurrentUserId()` to safely extract the authenticated user's ID from JWT claims.
*   **Standardized Responses**: Every endpoint returns a consistent JSON structure: `{ success: bool, message: string, data: object }`.
*   **State Delegation**: The controller focuses on HTTP concerns (routing, status codes, auth) while delegating all business logic and state transitions to the `_competitionService`.
*   **Security**: Uses the `[Authorize]` attribute on all state-changing actions (creating, accepting, submitting) to ensure only authenticated users can interact with the competition.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/CommunityController.cs ====
The `CommunityController.cs` file defines an API controller for managing a community platform, including posts, comments, exam-focused content, and moderation.

### **Key Class**
*   **`CommunityController`**: A `[ApiController]` that inherits from `ControllerBase`. It handles HTTP requests related to community interactions and delegates business logic to an `ICommunityService`.

### **Core Logic & Functionality**

#### **1. Post Management**
*   **CRUD Operations**: Methods to fetch all posts (paginated), get a specific post by ID, create, update, and delete posts.
*   **Interactions**: Allows users to **Like**, **Unlike**, and **Dislike** (downvote) posts.
*   **Authorization**: Creation, modification, and voting require a valid JWT token; user IDs are extracted from the `userId` claim.

#### **2. Comment Management**
*   **CRUD Operations**: Methods to get comments for a specific post, add new comments, and delete comments.
*   **Engagement**: Supports upvoting comments.
*   **Answer Marking**:
    *   **`MarkAsAnswer`**: Restricted to the `Teacher` role; marks a comment as a professional/authoritative answer.
    *   **`MarkBestAnswer`**: Allows the post author (or authorized user) to designate a specific comment as the best answer.

#### **3. Exam-Focused Features**
*   **Exam Questions & Tips**: Endpoints to retrieve exam-related questions and tips, filterable by subject or university.
*   **Doubt Solving**: A dedicated endpoint for retrieving community "doubts" (questions), supporting filters for solved/unsolved status.

#### **4. Search and User History**
*   **Global Search**: Allows searching across posts, comments, questions, and tips with filters for university and course IDs.
*   **User Activity**: Private endpoints for users to retrieve their own post and comment history.

#### **5. Moderation System**
*   **Reporting**: Users with `Teacher` or `Admin` roles can report problematic posts.
*   **Admin Dashboard**:
    *   **`GetReportedPosts`**: Allows Admins to view a list of flagged content.
    *   **`ResolvePostReport`**: Allows Admins to take action (e.g., dismiss, remove) on reported posts.

### **Technical Implementation Details**
*   **Service Pattern**: Relies entirely on `ICommunityService` for data operations.
*   **Data Transfer**: Uses various DTOs (e.g., `CreatePostDTO`, `ReportContentDTO`, `ResolveReportDTO`) for structured input validation.
*   **Response Format**: Consistently returns JSON objects containing `success`, `message`, and `data`/`post` fields.
*   **Note on Incompleteness**: The `UpdateComment` endpoint and some exam-focused filters are explicitly noted in the code as needing further implementation or service interface alignment.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/CompetitionsController.cs ====
This `CompetitionsController` manages the lifecycle, participation, and administration of academic or clan-based competitions.

### **Key Class: `CompetitionsController`**
*   **Base Class:** `ControllerBase`
*   **Dependencies:** `ICompetitionService` (Core logic) and `IClanService` (Clan/Role validation).
*   **Base Route:** `api/competitions`

---

### **Key Functions & Logic Groups**

#### **1. Discovery & Metadata**
*   **`GetCreateContext`**: Provides authorized users with a list of clans they lead/co-lead, helping the frontend populate competition creation forms.
*   **`GetAllCompetitions` / `GetCompetition`**: Standard public endpoints for browsing available contests with pagination.

#### **2. Competition Management (CRUD)**
*   **`CreateCompetition`**: Allows Students, Teachers, or Admins to propose new competitions.
*   **`UpdateCompetition` / `DeleteCompetition`**: Restrictive management tools for Teachers and Admins to modify or remove contests.

#### **3. Question Access Logic (Codeforces-style)**
*   **`GetAdminQuestions`**: Allows creators/admins to view questions at any time (pre-contest setup).
*   **`GetParticipantQuestions`**: Strictly limits question visibility to registered participants and only while the competition status is **"Ongoing"**.

#### **4. Participation & Interaction**
*   **`JoinCompetition` / `LeaveCompetition`**: Manages user registration/deregistration for specific contests.
*   **`SubmitCompetitionAnswers`**: Accepts and processes user submissions for grading.
*   **`GetMyCompetitions`**: Returns a list of contests the current user is involved in.

#### **5. Results & Analytics**
*   **`GetCompetitionLeaderboard`**: Provides a paged ranking of participants.
*   **`GetCompetitionStats`**: Returns high-level metrics about the competition's performance and participation.

#### **6. Administrative Workflow**
*   **`ApproveCompetition` / `RejectCompetition`**: Implements a vetting system where Admins must review and authorize user-submitted competitions before they go live.
*   **`GetPendingCompetitions`**: A specialized view for Admins to see contests awaiting approval.

---

### **Core Logic Summary**
*   **Role-Based Access Control (RBAC):** Uses `[Authorize]` with specific roles (Admin, Teacher, Student) to gate-keep management versus participation.
*   **State-Dependent Visibility:** Logic is baked into the service calls to ensure participants cannot see questions before the start time.
*   **Identity Integration:** Extensively extracts `userId` from JWT claims to validate ownership and participation status.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/CoursesController.cs ====
The `CoursesController.cs` file is a RESTful API controller in an ASP.NET Core application responsible for managing course-related operations. It acts as an orchestration layer between the client and the `ICourseService` and `IReviewService`.

### **Key Class**
*   **`CoursesController`**: Inherits from `ControllerBase`. It uses Dependency Injection to interact with `ICourseService` (for core course logic) and `IReviewService` (for handling course ratings).

### **Key Logic & Features**
*   **Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)**: Uses the `[Authorize]` attribute to restrict certain actions to specific roles like `Teacher` or `Admin`.
*   **Pagination & Filtering**: Most "GET" methods support query parameters for searching, sorting, and paginating results.
*   **Validation**: Validates `ModelState` and specific business rules (e.g., requiring a preview video when creating a course).
*   **User Context**: Frequently extracts `userId` and `Role` from JWT `Claims` to ensure users can only modify their own content.

### **Key Functions (Endpoints)**

#### **1. Course Discovery & Retrieval**
*   **`GetAllCourses` / `SearchCourses` / `FilterCourses`**: Provide various ways to find courses based on keywords, university, department, difficulty, and status.
*   **`GetCourse`**: Retrieves detailed information for a single course, including user-specific enrollment status.
*   **Specialized Lists**: Endpoints to fetch `Popular`, `Trending`, `New`, or courses specifically by `University`, `Department`, or `Teacher`.

#### **2. Course Management (Authorized)**
*   **`CreateCourse` (Teacher)**: Allows teachers to submit new courses for approval.
*   **`UpdateCourse` (Teacher/Admin)**: Allows modification of existing courses.
*   **`UpdateCourseStatus` (Admin)**: Allows administrators to approve or reject courses.
*   **`DeleteCourse` (Teacher/Admin)**: Removes a course from the system.

#### **3. User Interactions**
*   **`EnrollInCourse`**: Enrolls the authenticated user in a specific course.
*   **`GetMyCourses`**: Returns courses the current user is currently learning.
*   **`GetCreatedCourses`**: Returns courses authored by the current user (if they are a teacher).
*   **`SubmitRating`**: Processes course reviews and star ratings using the `IReviewService`.

#### **4. Content & Analytics**
*   **`GetCourseModules`**: Fetches the structural units (modules) of a course.
*   **`GetCourseReviews`**: Retrieves paginated feedback from other students.
*   **`GetCourseStats`**: Provides data insights (likely enrollment counts or performance metrics) for a specific course.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/DepartmentRequestsController.cs ====
The `DepartmentRequestsController` is a C# Web API controller responsible for handling requests to create new departments within universities.

### Class: `DepartmentRequestsController`
*   **Attributes**: `[ApiController]`, `[Route("api/[controller]")]`.
*   **Purpose**: Manages the API endpoints for department-related requests.
*   **Dependencies**: 
    *   `IDepartmentRequestService`: Injected service that handles the business logic for department requests.
    *   `ILogger`: Injected for logging request information, warnings, and errors.

### Key Function: `Create`
*   **Endpoint**: `POST api/DepartmentRequests`
*   **Input**: Accepts a `CreateDepartmentRequestDTO` from the request body.
*   **Logic**:
    1.  **Input Validation**: Checks if the DTO is null, verifies `UniversityId` is positive, and ensures `DepartmentName` is not empty. It also checks `ModelState.IsValid`.
    2.  **User Identification**: Extracts the `userId` from the `ClaimsPrincipal` (User identity) if the requester is authenticated.
    3.  **Service Invocation**: Calls `_requestService.CreateRequest(dto, userId)` to process the request.
    4.  **Error Handling**: If the service operation fails, it logs a warning and returns a `400 BadRequest` with the service's error message.
    5.  **Success Response**: On success, it logs the new request ID and returns a `200 Ok` containing the `requestId`.

### Summary of logic
The controller serves as an entry point that enforces basic validation and security context (extracting the user ID) before delegating the core operation to the service layer. It follows standard ASP.NET Core patterns for dependency injection, logging, and RESTful response handling.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/DepartmentsController.cs ====
This `DepartmentsController` is an ASP.NET Core API controller that manages department-related operations, primarily serving as a read-only interface that delegates business logic to an `IDepartmentService`.

### **Key Components & Logic**

*   **Service Integration:** Uses dependency injection to interact with `IDepartmentService` for data retrieval.
*   **Data Access (Read Operations):**
    *   **`GetAllDepartments`**: Retrieves departments. Supports filtering by `universityId` or paginated listing of all departments.
    *   **`GetDepartment(id)`**: Fetches detailed information for a specific department.

    *   **`GetDepartmentStats`**: Provides statistical data for a specific department.
    *   **`SearchDepartments`**: Implements search functionality by query string, with optional `universityId` filtering.
*   **Restricted Write Operations:**
    *   **`CreateDepartment`**, **`UpdateDepartment`**, and **`DeleteDepartment`**: Although marked with `[Authorize(Roles = "Admin")]`, these endpoints are **intentionally disabled**. They return a `Forbid` result, directing users toward a "Department Request" approval workflow instead of direct modification.

### **Return Pattern**
Every endpoint returns a consistent JSON structure containing a `success` boolean, an optional `message`, and the requested data (e.g., `departments`, `courses`, or `stats`). Errors are returned with `BadRequest` or `NotFound` statuses.

==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/NotificationsController.cs ====
The `NotificationsController` is an ASP.NET Core API controller that manages user notifications. It requires authentication for all endpoints and relies on an `INotificationService` to perform business logic.

### **Core Components**
- **Class:** `NotificationsController` (Inherits from `ControllerBase`).
- **Route:** `api/Notifications`.
- **Dependency:** `INotificationService` (Injected via constructor).
- **Authentication:** All methods are protected by the `[Authorize]` attribute.

### **Key Functions & Logic**

#### **1. Identity Management**
Most methods begin by extracting the `userId` from the JWT claims:
```csharp
var userId = int.Parse(User.FindFirst("userId")?.Value ?? "0");
```
It performs a safety check to ensure the token is valid before proceeding.

#### **2. Retrieval Endpoints**
- **`GetNotifications` (GET):** Fetches a paginated list of notifications.
  - **Parameters:** `unreadOnly` (optional filter), `page`, and `pageSize`.
  - **Logic:** Returns a list of notification DTOs for the current user.
- **`GetUnreadCount` (GET `/count`):** Returns the integer count of unread notifications specifically.

#### **3. Update Logic**
- **`MarkAsRead` (PUT `/{notificationId}/read`):** Marks a specific notification as read.
  - **Logic:** Ensures the notification belongs to the authenticated user before updating.
- **`MarkAllAsRead` (PUT `/read-all`):** Marks every unread notification for the user as read in bulk.

#### **4. Deletion**
- **`DeleteNotification` (DELETE `/{notificationId}`):** Removes a specific notification.
  - **Logic:** Verifies ownership before deletion.

### **Result Pattern**
The controller consistently returns JSON objects following this structure:
- **Success:** `Ok(new { success = true, notifications = ... / count = ... / message = ... })`
- **Failure:** `BadRequest(new { success = false, message = ... })` or `Unauthorized`.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/PaymentsController.cs ====
This is a summary of the `PaymentsController.cs` file, which serves as the primary API entry point for handling financial transactions, teacher earnings, and administrative payment management.

### **Overview**
- **Namespace:** `backend.Controllers`
- **Base Class:** `ControllerBase`
- **Dependencies:** Injects `IPaymentService` for business logic.
- **Security:** Globally protected by `[Authorize]`, with specific endpoints using `[AllowAnonymous]` (webhooks) or role-based access (`Admin`, `Teacher`).

---

### **Key Logic & Functionality Groups**

#### **1. Course Payments & Checkout**
*   **`InitiateCoursePayment`**: Starts a payment process for a specific course using `InitiatePaymentDTO`.
*   **`GetCourseCheckout`**: Retrieves checkout details (pricing, taxes, etc.) for a course before purchase.

#### **2. Payment Processing & Verification**
*   **`ProcessPayment`**: An **anonymous** endpoint designed for external payment gateways (webhooks) to notify the system of transaction results.
*   **`VerifyPayment`**: Allows a client to manually request verification of a specific transaction.

#### **3. User Payment History & Invoices**
*   **`GetPaymentHistory`**: Returns a paginated list of the user's past payments with filters for status, date range, and method.
*   **`GetPayment`**: Fetches detailed information for a single transaction.
*   **`GetInvoice` / `SendInvoice`**: Generates or emails PDF/data invoices for specific purchases.

#### **4. Refund System**
*   **`InitiateRefund` (Admin Only)**: Allows administrators to start a refund for a payment.
*   **`GetRefundStatus`**: Allows users to check the progress of a refund.
*   **`ProcessRefund` (Admin Only)**: Finalizes and executes a pending refund.

#### **5. Teacher Earnings & Withdrawals**
*   **`GetTeacherEarnings` / `GetEarningsSummary`**: Provides teachers with a breakdown of their income and current balances.
*   **`RequestWithdrawal`**: Allows teachers to request their available balance be paid out.
*   **`GetWithdrawalHistory`**: Lists past and pending withdrawal requests for the teacher.

#### **6. Administrative Management**
*   **`GetAllPayments`**: A master list of all platform transactions.
*   **`GetPlatformEarnings`**: Provides analytical data on platform revenue over daily, weekly, or monthly timeframes.
*   **`GetPendingWithdrawals` / `ApproveWithdrawal` / `RejectWithdrawal`**: Tools for admins to review and process payout requests from teachers.

#### **7. Payment Method Management**
*   **`GetAvailablePaymentMethods`**: Lists methods supported by the platform (e.g., Stripe, PayPal).
*   **`GetSavedPaymentMethods` / `SavePaymentMethod`**: Management for user-stored cards or accounts for faster checkout.

---

### **Implementation Patterns**
- **User Identification:** Consistently extracts `userId` from JWT claims (`User.FindFirst("userId")`).
- **Response Format:** Wraps service responses in a standard JSON object containing a `success` boolean, a `message`, and the `data` payload.
- **Validation:** Uses `ModelState.IsValid` to ensure DTOs meet requirements before passing them to the service layer.
The provided code is the `PaymentsController` for a .NET Web API, responsible for managing the financial and transactional aspects of an E-Learning platform. It acts as an interface between the client and the `IPaymentService`.

### **Core Functionality**

1.  **Saved Payment Methods**
    *   **`RemoveSavedPaymentMethod`**: Allows a user to delete a stored payment method (e.g., a credit card reference) from their profile.

2.  **Payment Statistics**
    *   **`GetUserPaymentStats`**: Provides a summary of spending and payment history for a specific student.
    *   **`GetPlatformPaymentStats`**: (Admin only) Provides high-level financial reporting for the entire platform, filterable by date range.

3.  **Coupon & Discount System**
    *   **`ApplyCoupon`**: Applies a discount code to a user's transaction.
    *   **`ValidateCoupon`**: Checks if a specific coupon code is valid and active before a purchase.
    *   **`CreateCoupon`**: (Admin/Teacher only) Allows authorized users to generate new discount codes.

4.  **Transaction Management**
    *   **`GetTransactionHistory`**: Returns a paginated list of all financial actions (payments, refunds, withdrawals) for the logged-in user.
    *   **`ExportPayments`**: (Admin only) Retrieves historical payment data for export (formatted as CSV, Excel, or PDF).

5.  **Administrative Settings**
    *   **`GetPaymentSettings`** / **`UpdatePaymentSettings`**: (Admin only) Manages global platform payment configurations, such as currency settings, gateway keys, or tax rates.

6.  **Support System**
    *   **`CreatePaymentSupportTicket`**: Allows users to open a dispute or support request regarding a specific payment issue.
    *   **`GetPaymentSupportTickets`**: Retrieves a history of a user's support tickets with status filtering and pagination.

### **Key Logic and Patterns**

*   **Security & Authorization**: 
    *   The controller uses the `[Authorize]` attribute extensively.
    *   Role-based access control (RBAC) restricts sensitive data (stats, settings, exports) to `Admin` or `Teacher` roles.
    *   User identity is verified by extracting a `userId` claim from the JWT token.
*   **Service-Oriented Architecture**: The controller contains minimal business logic; it delegates all operations to an injected `_paymentService`, which returns standardized result objects containing `Success`, `Message`, and `Data`.
*   **Data Handling**: 
    *   Uses specialized **DTOs** (Data Transfer Objects) like `ApplyCouponDTO` and `PaymentSettingsDTO` for structured data input and validation.
    *   Implements **Pagination** for transactions and support tickets to ensure efficient data transfer.
*   **Error Handling**: Returns consistent HTTP status codes (`200 OK`, `400 BadRequest`, `401 Unauthorized`) with JSON bodies indicating the success status and relevant error messages.


==== Summary for ./backend/Controllers/ProgressController.cs ====
The `ProgressController` manages student learning progress, tracking how much of a lesson a user has watched and calculating overall course completion percentages.

### **Core Class**
*   **`ProgressController`**: An API controller that interacts with the `ApplicationDbContext` to manage lesson-level and course-level progress for authenticated users.

### **Key Endpoints**
*   **`Watch` (`POST /api/lesson/{lessonId}/watch`)**: 
    *   Updates the `WatchedSeconds` for a specific lesson. 
    *   **Logic**: If the watched time reaches 90% of the lesson's duration, it automatically marks the lesson as `IsCompleted`.
*   **`Complete` (`POST /api/lesson/{lessonId}/complete`)**: 
    *   Manually marks a lesson as completed regardless of watch time.
*   **`GetCourseProgress` (`GET /api/course/{courseId}/progress`)**: 
    *   Returns a summary of total vs. completed lessons and the overall percentage for a specific course.
*   **`GetLessonStatus` (`GET /api/course/{courseId}/lesson-status`)**: 
    *   Provides a detailed list of all lessons in a course with their individual completion status and watched time.

### **Key Logic & Helpers**
*   **`UpdateEnrollmentProgress` (Private)**: 
    *   Triggered after a lesson is completed. 
    *   Recalculates the total and completed lesson count for the course enrollment. 
    *   Updates the `ProgressPercentage` and sets the enrollment status to `"Completed"` if it reaches 100%.
*   **`GetCurrentUserId` (Private)**: 
    *   Extracts the user ID from JWT claims (`NameIdentifier`, `id`, or `sub`).
*   **Persistence**: 
    *   Progress is stored in the `StudentLessonProgresses` table.
    *   The `Enrollments` table is updated to reflect high-level course completion stats for quick retrieval elsewhere in the app.

*   **Authorization**: Both endpoints require the user to have either the **Teacher** or **Admin** role.
*   **File Management**: 
    *   Automatically creates the target directories if they do not exist.
    *   Uses GUIDs to prevent filename collisions.
    *   Uses `IWebHostEnvironment` to resolve the physical path of the `wwwroot` folder.
*   **Validation**: Performs server-side checks on file presence, size, and metadata (duration) before processing.

#### **4. Timing & Logic**
- **`SubmittedAt`**: Automatically defaults to `DateTime.UtcNow`.

- **Resubmission Logic**: Tracks if the entry `IsResubmission` and maintains a reference to the `OriginalSubmissionId`.

==== Summary for ./backend/Models/ClanAnnouncement.cs ====
This code defines the Entity Framework Core models for managing clan announcements and reactions within the system.

### **Key Classes & Models**

*   **`ClanAnnouncement`**: Represents a message posted within a specific clan.
    *   **Logic**: Tracks the creator (`UserId`), parent clan (`ClanId`), content, and metadata like `Type` (General, Event, etc.), `IsPinned` status, and `ViewCount`.
    *   **Audit**: Automatically sets `CreatedAt` to UTC now.
*   **`ClanAnnouncementReaction`**: Handles emoji-based interactions (e.g., 👍, ❤️) for clan announcements.
    *   **Logic**: Links a specific user and emoji to an announcement.
*   **`PostReaction`**: A similar entity for managing emoji reactions on community `Post` entities.

### **Database Logic & Structure**
*   **Attributes**: Uses Data Annotations (`[Table]`, `[Key]`, `[Required]`, `[MaxLength]`) to define schema constraints and table names (`ClanAnnouncements`, `ClanAnnouncementReactions`, `PostReactions`).
*   **Relationships**: 
    *   Announcements are linked via foreign keys to both `Clan` and `User`.
    *   Reactions maintain many-to-one relationships with their respective parent content (Announcement or Post) and the reacting User.
    *   Uses `ICollection` for lazy-loading or including reactions in announcement queries.

==== Summary for ./backend/Models/ClanVsClansCompetition.cs ====
The provided code defines the `ClanVsClansCompetition` model, which represents a competitive event between two clans in the E-Learning platform.

### **Core Class: `ClanVsClansCompetition`**
This class serves as a database entity (mapped to the `ClanVsClansCompetitions` table) that manages the lifecycle and configuration of a dual-clan competition.

### **Key Components & Logic**

*   **Clan Identification:** Tracks two specific clans: the **Challenger** and the **Opponent**.
*   **Competition Configuration:**
    *   **Type:** Supports different modes like `Programming`, `Quiz`, or `Mixed`.
    *   **Difficulty:** Categorized as `Easy`, `Medium`, or `Hard`.
    *   **Parameters:** Defines the number of participants per clan and the duration (in minutes).
*   **Status Management:** Tracks the state of the competition via a `Status` field (e.g., `Pending`, `Scheduled`, `Ongoing`, `Completed`, `Rejected`, `Cancelled`).
*   **Workflow Logic:**
    *   **Readiness:** Boolean flags (`ChallengerReady`, `OpponentReady`) track when both sides have finalized their participant lists.
    *   **Response:** Captures the opponent's reaction to the challenge (`Accepted`, `Rejected`).
*   **Results & Scoring:**
    *   Stores final totals for both clans (`ChallengerTotalScore`, `OpponentTotalScore`).
    *   Identifies the winner via `WinnerClanStatus` (`ChallengerWon`, `OpponentWon`, or `Draw`).
*   **Navigation Properties:**
    *   Links to the `Clan` entities for both challenger and opponent.
    *   Links to the `User` who created the competition.
    *   Includes collections for **Participants**, **Scores**, and **Questions** specific to the competition instance.
*   **Content Reference:** Uses a `QuestionIds` string (comma-separated) to reference the specific questions selected for the event.

==== Summary for ./backend/Models/Competition.cs ====
The `Competition.cs` file defines the **`Competition`** class within the `backend.Models` namespace. It serves as a comprehensive data model for managing various types of educational and competitive events (Quizzes, Programming, etc.) across different scopes of the platform.

### **Key Components**

#### **1. Core Identification & Metadata**
*   **`Id`**, **`Title`**, **`Description`**: Standard fields for identifying and describing the competition.
*   **`CompetitionType`**: Defines the format (e.g., Quiz, Assignment, Programming, Essay, Debate).
*   **`Status`**: Tracks the lifecycle (Upcoming, Ongoing, Completed, Cancelled, PendingApproval).
*   **`Season`**: Used for season-specific leaderboards and point tracking.

#### **2. Scope & Hierarchy**
The model supports multi-level organization through optional foreign keys:
*   **`UniversityId`**, **`DepartmentId`**, **`CourseId`**, **`ClanId`**: Allows competitions to be scoped to specific institutions, academic departments, individual courses, or student clans.

#### **3. Timing & Rules**
*   **Timing**: Includes `StartDate`, `EndDate`, `DurationMinutes`, and an `IsLive` flag.
*   **Participation Rules**: Handles `MaxParticipants`, team-based settings (`IsTeamBased`, `TeamSize`), and attempt limits.
*   **Visibility/Access**: Supports public or private access (`IsPublic`, `AllowedMemberIds`, `AllowedClanIds`) and point-based entry requirements for clan members.

#### **4. Content & Scoring**
*   **Content Links**: Can link directly to an existing `QuizId` or `AssignmentId`.
*   **Scoring**: Tracks `TotalQuestions`, `MaxScore`, and a JSON-based `ScoringSystem`. It also determines if a leaderboard should be displayed.
*   **Ranking Rewards**: Defines points awarded for first, second, and third place, as well as participation points.

#### **5. Prizes & Incentives**
*   **Rewards**: Fields for `PrizeDescription`, `PrizeAmount` (decimal), `PrizeBadge`, and `PrizePoints`.

#### **6. Administrative Workflow**
*   **Ownership**: Tracks the `CreatorId` and `CreatorRole` (Student, Teacher, Admin, or ClanLeader).
*   **Approval**: Includes an `IsApproved` flag (defaulting to false for students) and tracks who approved it and when.

#### **7. Navigation Properties (Relationships)**
*   **Belongs to (Foreign Keys)**: `Creator` (User), `University`, `Department`, `Course`, `Clan`, `Quiz`, and `Assignment`.
*   **Has many (Collections)**:
    *   `Participants`: List of users/teams joined.
    *   `Scores`: Recorded results for participants.
    *   `Questions`: Specific questions associated with this competition.

### **Key Components**

*   **`UniversityId` & `DepartmentId`**: Places the course within a specific academic structure.
*   **`TeacherId`**: Links the course to its creator (User).

#### **2. Educational Content & Exam Focus**
*   **Core Info**: Title, descriptions (short/full), and difficulty levels.
*   **Exam Features**: Specific fields for `ExamFocus`, `ExamPattern` (MCQ, Written), and JSON arrays for `ImportantTopics` and `PreviousQuestions`.
*   **Metadata**: Tracks totals for modules, lessons, quizzes, assignments, and duration.

#### **3. Pricing & Monetization**
*   **`IsFree`**: Toggle for free vs. paid content.
*   **Financials**: Manages `Price`, `DiscountPrice`, and automatically calculates `PlatformCommission` (10% default) and `TeacherEarning`.

#### **4. Lifecycle & Governance**
*   **`Status`**: State management (Pending, Approved, Rejected, Published, Draft).
*   **Approval**: Tracks who approved the course (`ApprovedBy`), when (`ApprovedAt`), or why it was rejected (`RejectionReason`).

*   **Discovery**: Includes `Tags` and `Keywords` for search optimization.

#### **6. Navigation Properties (Relationships)**
The model acts as a hub, connecting to:
*   **Parents**: `University`, `Department`, and `Teacher` (User).
*   **Children (Collections)**: `Modules`, `CourseParts`, `Enrollments`, `Reviews`, `Quizzes`, `Assignments`, `Posts`, and `Competitions`.

### **Logic Summary**
This class serves as a **Data Transfer Object (DTO) mapping** and **Database Schema definition** (via Entity Framework Core attributes like `[Table]`, `[Key]`, and `[ForeignKey]`). It enforces data integrity for a complex educational marketplace, balancing academic hierarchy with commercial features.

### **Key Class & Structure**
*   **`Payment` Class**: A data model representing a transaction. It uses Data Annotations for schema configuration (e.g., `[Table]`, `[Key]`, `[Required]`, `[MaxLength]`).

### **Key Logic & Categories**

#### **1. Identification & Relationships**
*   **Unique Identifiers**: Includes an `Id` (Primary Key) and a `TransactionId` for tracking external payment provider records.
*   **Core Associations**: Connects a payment to a **`User`** (via `UserId`) and optionally to a **`Course`** (via `CourseId`).

#### **2. Financial Breakdown & Commission Logic**
*   **Platform Split**: Tracks the division of funds between **`PlatformCommission`**, **`TeacherEarning`**, and **`PlatformEarning`**.
*   **Total Value**: Captures the `Amount`, `TaxAmount`, `DiscountAmount`, and defaults the `Currency` to **BDT**.

#### **3. Status & Workflow Tracking**
*   **Lifecycle Management**: Uses the `Status` property to move through states: *Pending, Processing, Completed, Failed, Refunded, Cancelled*.
*   **Provider Metadata**: Stores the provider name (`PaymentProvider`) and the raw JSON response (`PaymentDetails`) from external gateways like Stripe or SSLCommerz.

#### **4. Refund & Invoice System**
*   **Refund Logic**: Includes flags and fields to track if a payment was refunded, the reason, the amount, and the timestamp.
*   **Invoicing**: Stores references to generated `InvoiceNumber` and external `InvoiceUrl`.

#### **5. Temporal Tracking**
*   **Timestamps**: Automatically captures `CreatedAt` (defaulting to UTC) and tracks completion or expiration dates for time-sensitive transactions.

==== Summary for ./backend/Services/AuthService.cs ====
This file contains the **`AuthService`** class, which implements the `IAuthService` interface. It handles user authentication, profile management, and role transitions within the E-Learning platform.

### **Core Functions**

#### **1. Authentication**
*   **`Register`**: Creates a new user account with the "Student" role by default. It hashes passwords using `IPasswordHasher` and returns a JWT token upon successful creation.
*   **`Login`**: Validates credentials against the database. If successful, it updates the `LastLogin` timestamp and generates a new JWT token.
*   **`ForgotPassword` & `ResetPassword`**: Manages the password recovery flow by generating secure, time-limited reset tokens (valid for 1 hour).

#### **2. Profile Management**
*   **`GetUserProfile`**: Retrieves detailed user information, including current **Clan membership** details (role, contribution points, and clan metadata).
*   **`UpdateProfile`**: Allows users to update personal details such as names, contact info, bio, and URLs for profile/cover images.
*   **`ChangePassword`**: Validates the current password before allowing a user to set a new one.

#### **3. Role & Status Management**
*   **`BecomeTeacher`**: Submits a request for a user to become a teacher. It sets a `TeacherPendingApproval` flag for administrative review rather than granting the role immediately.
*   **`GetUserDashboard`**: Compiles a comprehensive view for the user, including:
    *   Basic profile and Clan data.
    *   **Recent Enrollments**: Includes progress tracking logic that calculates completed lessons based on percentage if explicit counts are missing.
    *   **Recommended Courses**: Fetches popular approved courses.

### **Key Logic & Implementation Details**
*   **Security**: Uses an injected `IPasswordHasher` for credential security and `IJwtHelper` for token generation.
*   **Data Integrity**: Employs `ServiceResult<T>` patterns to return standardized success or failure messages across the service.
*   **Performance**: Uses `.AsNoTracking()` and specific projections (`.Select(...)`) when fetching profile and dashboard data to reduce memory usage and improve query speed.
*   **Legacy Data Handling**: In `GetUserDashboard`, the service includes fallback logic to estimate lesson progress for older enrollment records by cross-referencing `CourseParts`.

    *   **`CreateClan`**: Facilitates clan creation while enforcing a "Leadership Exclusivity" rule (users cannot create a clan if they already hold a `Leader` or `CoLeader` role elsewhere). The creator is automatically assigned as the initial `Leader`.
    *   **`UpdateClan`**: Allows `Leader` or `CoLeader` roles to modify clan metadata (motto, description, privacy settings, etc.).
    *   **`DeleteClan`**: Restricts deletion rights exclusively to the clan `Leader`, removing all member records before deleting the clan entity.

*   **Discovery & Search:**
    *   **`SearchClans`**: Implements a robust filtering system supporting text search (name/tag/description), University/Department IDs, clan types, ranking ranges, and member counts.
    *   **`GetAllClans`**: Returns a paginated list of clans ordered by their `TotalPoints`.
    *   **`GetClanById`**: Fetches detailed information for a specific clan, including the current user's membership status or pending join requests.

*   **Membership & Hierarchy:**
    *   **`JoinClan`**: Manages the join workflow. If a clan is public and requires no approval, the user is added immediately; otherwise, it generates a `Pending` join request. It also enforces `MaxMembers` capacity limits.
    *   **`LeaveClan`**: (Partial) Handles member departures, with logic to prevent a `Leader` from leaving if no `CoLeader` is available to take over.
    *   **`GetClanMembers`**: Retrieves a list of members ordered by hierarchy (`Leader` > `CoLeader` > `Member`) and then by contribution points.

### **Key Logic & Constraints**
*   **Role-Based Access Control (RBAC):** Critical operations (update, delete) check the actor's role against the `ClanMembers` table to ensure authorization.
*   **Approval Workflow:** The service distinguishes between `IsPublic` and `RequireApproval` flags to determine if a user enters a clan immediately or enters a "Pending" state.
*   **Data Integrity:** Uses `AsQueryable` for efficient server-side filtering and sorting before executing database queries.
*   **Serialization:** Complex fields like `FocusSubjects` are stored as JSON strings in the database using `JsonSerializer`.

This code implements a **post voting system** (likely within a service class) that manages upvotes and downvotes for a community platform.

### Key Components

*   **Primary Logic:** `VotePostAsync` (implied method name)
*   **Data Models:** Operates on `Post` (to update aggregate counts) and `PostVote` (to track individual user selections).
*   **Return Type:** Returns a `ServiceResult<bool>`, indicating whether the operation succeeded or failed with an error message.

### Core Functions & Logic

1.  **Toggle Mechanism:**
    *   If a user clicks the **same vote button** they previously pressed (e.g., upvoting an already upvoted post), the vote is **removed** (deleted from the database).
    *   If a user clicks the **opposite vote button**, the existing vote is **updated**, and the post's counts are adjusted accordingly.

2.  **Vote Count Synchronization:**
    *   Manages two counters on the `Post` object: `UpvoteCount` and `DownvoteCount`.
    *   Automatically increments or decrements these counters based on whether a vote is added, removed, or changed to ensure the aggregate totals stay in sync with individual `PostVote` records.

3.  **New Vote Creation:**
    *   If no previous vote exists, it creates a new `PostVote` entry with the user ID, post ID, and vote type (1 for upvote, typically -1 or 2 for downvote), and sets the `CreatedAt` timestamp to `UtcNow`.

4.  **Error Handling & Validation:**
    *   **Validation:** Checks if the post exists before attempting to vote; returns a "Post not found" failure if it doesn't.
    *   **Exception Handling:** Uses a `try-catch` block to capture database or runtime errors, returning a failure result with the specific exception message.

This code file defines the **`ClanVsClansCompetitionService`**, which manages the lifecycle of competitive challenges between clans in the E-Learning platform.

### **Core Class & Interface**
*   **`IClanVsClansCompetitionService`**: The interface defining the contract for managing competitions, participant selection, scoring, and results.
*   **`ClanVsClansCompetitionService`**: The implementation class that interacts with `ApplicationDbContext` for data persistence and `INotificationService` for user alerts.

### **Key Logic & Functions**

#### **1. Challenge Management**
*   **`CreateCompetitionAsync`**: Allows clan leaders or co-leaders to challenge another clan. It validates clan existence, ensures the challenger isn't targeting their own clan, enforces constraints (participant limits, duration), and notifies the opponent's leadership.
*   **`AcceptChallengeAsync` / `RejectChallengeAsync`**: Handles the opponent clan's response. Accepting moves the status to "Scheduled," while rejecting marks it as "Rejected" and notifies the challenger.
*   **`GetPendingChallengesAsync`**: Specifically retrieves challenges that are waiting for a response from the clan.

#### **2. Authorization & Validation**
*   **`IsUserClanLeaderAsync`**: A private helper that verifies if a user has the "Leader" or "CoLeader" role within a specific clan before allowing administrative actions.
*   **Membership Verification**: Ensures that any selected participants are actually members of the participating clan.

#### **3. Participant Management**
*   **`SelectParticipantsAsync`**: Facilitates the selection of a specific number of clan members to represent the clan in the competition. It clears previous selections and updates the clan's "ready" status once the required number of participants is met.
*   **`ConfirmParticipantSelectionAsync`**: Updates the `ChallengerReady` or `OpponentReady` flags in the database.

#### **4. Retrieval & DTO Mapping**
*   **`GetCompetitionAsync` / `GetClanCompetitionsAsync`**: Retrieves detailed or list-based views of competitions, utilizing Eager Loading (`Include`) for related clan data and mapping entities to DTOs for the frontend.

### **Workflow Logic**
The service follows a state-machine pattern:
1.  **Pending**: Challenge created, waiting for opponent.
2.  **Scheduled / Rejected**: Opponent accepts or declines.
3.  **Participant Selection**: Both clans must select their teams and mark themselves as "Ready."
4.  **Execution** (implied by interface): Questions are fetched, answers submitted, and scores calculated.
This code implements the **`ClanVsClansCompetitionService`**, which manages the lifecycle, execution, and scoring of competitive matches between two clans.

### Key Functions

#### 1. Competition Lifecycle Management
*   **`ConfirmSelection`**: Finalizes participant selection for a clan. When both the challenger and opponent clans are ready, the competition status is updated to `"Ready"`.
*   **`StartCompetitionAsync`**: Transitions a competition from `"Ready"` to `"Ongoing"`. It records the start time and sets the status of all selected participants to `"Started"`.
*   **`CompleteCompetitionAsync`**: Marks an individual participant's progress as `"Completed"`. If all participants in the match have finished, it triggers the global result calculation.

#### 2. Question & Answer Logic
*   **`GetCompetitionQuestionsAsync`**: Retrieves the list of questions assigned to a specific competition, ordered by their sequence, and returns them as DTOs.
*   **`SubmitAnswerAsync`**: Validates a user's answer against the database. If correct, it increments the participant's `CorrectAnswers` count and adds the question's `Points` to their individual score.

#### 3. Scoring & Results
*   **`CalculateCompetitionResultsAsync` (Private)**: Aggregates the scores of all participants for both the Challenger and Opponent clans. It determines the winner (`ChallengerWon`, `OpponentWon`, or `Draw`) and updates the competition status to `"Completed"`.
*   **`GetCompetitionResultsAsync`**: Provides a comprehensive summary of a finished match, including total clan scores, winner status, and ranked individual performance for both sides.
*   **`GetClanParticipantResultsAsync`**: Returns a ranked list of results specifically for one clan's participants.

### Core Logic & Architecture
*   **State Machine**: The service manages complex status transitions for both the competition (`Scheduled` → `Ready` → `Ongoing` → `Completed`) and the individual participants (`Selected` → `Started` → `Completed`).
*   **Aggregation**: Winning is determined by the collective performance of all clan members rather than a single individual.
*   **Data Transformation**: Uses helper methods (`GetCompetitionDTOAsync`, `GetParticipantDTO`) to map complex database entities into clean Data Transfer Objects (DTOs) for the frontend.
*   **Error Handling**: Every public method is wrapped in a `try-catch` block returning a `ServiceResult<T>`, ensuring that database or logic failures are caught and returned as descriptive error messages.

### **File Summary: `CompetitionService.cs`**

This file contains the business logic for managing competitions within the E-Learning platform. It handles the creation, validation, access control, and status tracking of quiz-based contests.

---

### **1. Core Class**
*   **`CompetitionService`**: Implements `ICompetitionService`. It interacts with the `ApplicationDbContext` to manage competitions, questions, participants, and scores.

---

### **2. Key Logic & Workflows**

#### **Approval & Role System**
*   **Auto-Approval**: Competitions created by **Admins**, **Teachers**, or verified **Clan Leaders** are automatically approved (`IsApproved = true`).
*   **Student Requests**: Competitions created by Students default to `PendingApproval` and require manual review.
*   **Role Identification**: The service checks the `ClanMembers` table to verify if a user holds a "Leader" or "CoLeader" rank before granting elevated permissions.

#### **Access Control & Eligibility**
*   **Public vs. Private**: Supports public competitions or restricted ones.
*   **Targeting**: Private competitions can target specific users or entire clans.
*   **Point Filtering**: Allows creators to restrict eligibility based on a member's `TotalPoints` range (e.g., a competition only for students with 500–1000 points).

#### **Competition Lifecycle (Status Logic)**
The status is dynamically calculated based on the current UTC time and approval state:
*   **PendingApproval**: Not yet approved by an admin.
*   **Upcoming**: Approved but start time hasn't been reached.
*   **Ongoing**: Current time is between start and end dates.
*   **Completed**: End date has passed.

---

### **3. Key Functions**

| Function | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| **`CreateCompetition`** | Validates input, determines the creator's role, calculates the list of eligible members (merging manual IDs and clan filters), and saves the competition and questions to the database. |
| **`GetParticipantQuestions`** | **Strictly gated.** Only allows registered participants to view questions if the competition status is currently **Ongoing**. |
| **`GetAdminQuestions`** | Allows creators and system admins to view questions at any time (even before the contest starts) for management purposes. |
| **`GetClanMembersWithPointRange`**| A helper that filters members of specific clans by their point totals to build an "Allowed Members" list. |
| **`CalculateStatus`** | A utility to determine the competition state relative to `DateTime.UtcNow`. |

---

### **4. Database Integration**
*   **Entities Managed**: `Competition`, `CompetitionQuestion`, `ClanMember`, and `User`.
*   **Storage Strategy**: Stores allowed participant/clan lists as **CSV strings** in the database for efficient filtering during access checks.

### **Key Functions & Logic**

#### **1. Retrieval & Pagination**
- **`GetNewCourses`**: Fetches a list of newly added courses.
- **`GetCoursesByTeacher`**: Provides a paginated list of courses authored by a specific teacher, including metadata like enrollment counts, ratings, and status.

#### **2. Course Management (CRUD)**
- **`CreateCourse`**: 
    - Validates that the specified Department belongs to the University.
    - Maps `CourseCreateDTO` to the `Course` model.
    - Automatically persists associated **`VideoParts`** (lessons/segments) with specific ordering.
    - Sets initial status to `"Pending"`.
- **`UpdateCourse`**: 
    - Enforces authorization: Only the course author (teacher) or an Admin can edit.
    - Performs partial updates (only modifies provided fields).
    - Employs a **"simple replace" strategy** for video parts (removes existing parts and adds new ones if provided).
- **`UpdateCourseStatus` (Admin Only)**: 
    - Handles workflow transitions: `Approve` (sets to `Published`), `Reject` (requires a reason), `Pending`, or `Publish`.
    - Tracks who approved the course and when.

#### **3. Enrollment Logic**
- **`EnrollInCourse`**:
    - Validates course and user existence.
    - **Idempotency**: If a user is already enrolled, it returns the existing enrollment details instead of creating a duplicate.
    - **Initialization**: Creates a new `Enrollment` record with `"Active"` status and 0% progress.
    - **Counters**: Atomically increments the `EnrollmentCount` on the `Course` record.

### **Technical Implementation Details**
- **DTO Projection**: Extensively uses `.Select()` to map database entities to `CourseDTO` or `EnrollmentDTO`, ensuring sensitive or unnecessary data isn't sent to the client.
- **Eager Loading/Joins**: Accesses related data (e.g., `c.University.Name`, `c.Teacher.FirstName`) to provide rich response objects.
- **Error Handling**: Every method is wrapped in `try-catch` blocks, returning `ServiceResult.FailureResult` with the exception message on failure.

==== Summary for ./backend/Services/DepartmentRequestService.cs ====
This code file defines the **`DepartmentRequestService`** class within the `backend.Services` namespace. It manages the lifecycle of requests to add new departments to universities, handling validation, retrieval, approval, and rejection logic.

### Key Class
*   **`DepartmentRequestService`**: Implements `IDepartmentRequestService` and uses `ApplicationDbContext` for database operations.

### Key Functions
*   **`CreateRequest`**:
    *   Validates that the target university exists.
    *   Ensures no duplicate requests for the same department name exist within that university.
    *   Persists a new `DepartmentRequest` with a "Pending" status.
*   **`GetRequests`**:
    *   Retrieves a paginated list of requests.
    *   Supports optional filtering by `universityId` and `status`.
    *   Maps results to `DepartmentRequestAdminDTO`.
*   **`ApproveRequest`**:
    *   Validates the request exists and is currently "Pending."
    *   Checks if the department already exists in the `Departments` table to prevent duplicates.
    *   Automatically generates a `ShortCode` if one isn't provided (stripping non-alphanumeric characters and capping length).
    *   Creates a new `Department` entry and updates the request status to "Approved."
*   **`RejectRequest`**:
    *   Validates the request status.
    *   Updates the request status to "Rejected" and optionally updates the admin's `Note`.

### Core Logic & Patterns
*   **Validation**: Rigorous checks for existence and duplicates before performing write operations.
*   **Short Code Generation**: Contains fallback logic to derive a 5-character department code from its name if the requester left it blank.
*   **Encapsulation**: Uses a `ServiceResult<T>` wrapper to return success/failure states and error messages to the controller layer.
*   **Audit Trails**: Automatically sets `CreatedAt` and `ReviewedAt` timestamps using `DateTime.UtcNow`.

    *   Retrieves a paginated list of courses a user is enrolled in.

    *   Records or updates a `LessonProgress` entry as completed.

    *   Provides a detailed breakdown of progress for a specific user and course.

*   **Service Result Pattern**: Uses `ServiceResult<T>` to return either success data or error messages uniformly.
*   **DTO Mapping**: Manually maps `Enrollment` models to `EnrollmentDTO` for API responses.
*   **Resiliency**: Uses `try-catch` blocks around SignalR calls to ensure notification failures do not break the core database transaction.

- **Settings:** Provides administrative control over platform-wide payment settings.
- **Security:** Includes validation logic for payment-related security data.

#### **6. Marketing & Support**

- **Support:** Integrated ticketing system specifically for payment-related issues and bulk export of payment data for audits.

*   **`GetApplicationDetails(applicationId)`**: Fetches full details for a specific application, including applicant and reviewer information.
*   **`ReviewApplication(applicationId, reviewDto, adminId)`**: Processes an admin's decision on a request.
    *   **Decision Logic**: If "Approved," the applicant's `IsTeacher` flag is set to `true`.
    *   **Cleanup**: In all review outcomes (Approved or Rejected), the user's `TeacherPendingApproval` flag is cleared.

#### **3. Support Logic**
*   **`MapToDTO(application, user)`**: A private helper method that transforms the internal database models (`TeacherApplication` and `User`) into a flattened `TeacherApplicationDTO` for API responses.
*   **`GetUserById(userId)`**: A basic utility to fetch user details.

==== Summary for ./backend/Services/UniversityRequestService.cs ====
The `UniversityRequestService.cs` file implements the management of user-submitted requests to add new universities to the system.

### **Core Class**
*   **`UniversityRequestService`**: Implements `IUniversityRequestService` and uses `ApplicationDbContext` for database operations. It handles the lifecycle of a university request from creation to approval or rejection.

### **Key Functions**
*   **`CreateRequest`**: 
    *   Validates if a university with the same name already exists.
    *   Creates a new `UniversityRequest` entry with a "Pending" status.
*   **`GetRequests`**: 
    *   Retrieves a paginated list of requests.
    *   Supports optional filtering by status (e.g., Pending, Approved, Rejected).
*   **`ApproveRequest`**: 
    *   Validates that the request is "Pending."
    *   Generates a unique URL-friendly code for the university.
    *   Moves the data from the `UniversityRequest` table to the main `Universities` table.
    *   Marks the request as "Approved."
*   **`RejectRequest`**: 
    *   Updates the request status to "Rejected."
    *   Allows an optional administrative note to explain the rejection.
*   **`GenerateUniqueCodeAsync` (Private)**: 
    *   Converts the university name into a slug (lowercase, alphanumeric, and hyphens).
    *   Iteratively appends numeric suffixes (e.g., `-1`, `-2`) to ensure the code is unique within the database while maintaining a 50-character limit.

### **Key Logic & Constraints**
*   **Validation**: Prevents duplicate university entries by checking existing names (case-insensitive) during both the request and approval phases.
*   **State Management**: Ensures requests can only be approved or rejected if they are currently in a "Pending" state.
*   **Slug Generation**: Uses Regular Expressions (`Regex`) to clean names for use as unique identifiers/URLs.
*   **Concurrency/Persistence**: Uses `SaveChangesAsync` to ensure atomic updates to both the request status and the creation of the new university entity.

==== Summary for ./frontend/src/api/authContext.js ====
This code defines a React Context for managing and providing authentication state across the application.

### **Key Components**
*   **`AuthContext`**: A React Context object created to hold the user's authentication data and loading status.
*   **`AuthProvider`**: A provider component that wraps the application to manage auth state:
    *   **`user`**: Stores the profile data of the authenticated user.
    *   **`loading`**: A boolean flag indicating if the initial profile fetch is in progress.
*   **`useAuth`**: A custom hook that provides a convenient way for components to access the authentication context (`user`, `loading`, and `setUser`).

### **Logic & Execution**
*   **Token Retrieval**: On component mount, it attempts to retrieve an access token using `getAccessToken()`.
*   **Profile Fetching**: If a token is present, it executes an authenticated `GET` request to `/auth/profile` via `apiFetch`.
*   **State Updates**:
    *   If the request succeeds, `user` is updated with the profile data.
    *   If the request fails or no token exists, `user` remains `null`.
    *   `loading` is set to `false` once the check is complete, regardless of the outcome.


==== Summary for ./frontend/src/api/client.js ====
This code provides a centralized utility for making HTTP requests to a backend API, featuring automatic authentication handling and robust error parsing.

### Key Functions

*   **`apiFetch(path, options)`**: The primary exported function that wraps the native `fetch` API. It manages headers, stringifies request bodies, injects Bearer tokens if `auth` is enabled, and standardizes error handling for non-204 responses.
*   **`readJsonSafely(response)`**: A helper function that inspects the `Content-Type` header. It returns parsed JSON if available; otherwise, it returns the response as plain text to prevent parsing errors.

### Core Logic

*   **Base URL Configuration**: Uses `REACT_APP_API_BASE_URL` from environment variables, defaulting to `http://localhost:5145/api`.
*   **Authentication**: If the `auth` flag is set, it retrieves a token via `getAccessToken()` and attaches it to the `Authorization` header.
*   **Error Standardizing**: When a request fails (`!res.ok`), it extracts error messages from the payload (looking for `title` or `message` fields) and throws a custom `Error` object containing the HTTP status and the full payload.
*   **Response Handling**: Automatically returns `null` for `204 No Content` status codes and parses the body for all other successful responses.

### Logic
